Quick, high-impact notes to level this up: - **Pacing / shock fatigue:** long digressions (lists of drugs/insults, reporter tangent) blunt the blows.

Add crisp section signposts (“AGGIE,” “VISCERA,” “HALO”) at every switch, and anchor POV in the first two lines of each segment. - **Dialogue handling:** many speeches read like transcribed rants.

Break them with physical beats and reaction shots so we aren’t white-water rafting on paragraph-length monologues. - **Theme vs. edgelord:** the text intentionally leans into racism, misogyny, and sexual violence.

Make the *authorial stance* unmistakable via consequences and reflection beats—else some readers will read endorsement.

Consider promoting it earlier or ending a prior chapter on the shotgun click (or the can of beans flipping) to create a cleaner hinge into the origin. - **Line craft:** hunt filter words (“I remember,” “I saw,” “I think”), stack fewer adverbs, and swap some multi-clause sentences for short hammers to vary rhythm. ### Tiny before/after to show the tightening Original vibe (paraphrased): the beans tilt, flip “four and a half” times, entrails on the carpet, shriek, stitches seeping, ketchup like gore… Tightened sample (same meaning, less bloat): > The can tipped off his elbow, cartwheeled, and burst.
